public class Solution {
    public Dictionary<int,int>Dict= new Dictionary<int,int>();
    public int EvenCount = 0;
    public int OddCount = 0;
    public int[]Nums = [];
    public int MaxLength;
    public int LongestBalanced(int[] nums) {
        // edge cases
        // 
        Nums = nums;
        for(int i = 0; i<nums.Length;i++){
            var num = nums[i];
            if(Dict.TryGetValue(num,out var count)){
                Dict[num]=count+1;
            }else{
                Dict.Add(num,1);
                if(num%2==0){
                    EvenCount++;
                }else{
                    OddCount++;
                }
            }
            
            
        }
        if(EvenCount==OddCount){
            return nums.Length;
        }
        if(EvenCount==0 || OddCount==0){
            return 0;
        }
        GetLongest(1,nums.Length-1,nums[0]);

        GetLongest(0,nums.Length-2,nums[^1]);
        return MaxLength;



    }
    public void GetLongest(int start,int end,int num){
    //removing and counting
        (int evenCount,int oddCount) countSave = (EvenCount,OddCount);
        var maxLength = -1;
            var count =Dict[num];
            if(count>1){
                Dict[num]= count-1;
            }else{
                Dict.Remove(num);
                if(num%2==0){
                    EvenCount --;
                }else{
                    OddCount --;
                }
            }

        if(EvenCount==OddCount){
            MaxLength = end - start+1;
        }else if(end-start>MaxLength){
            if(end - (start+1)!=0){
                GetLongest(start+1,end,Nums[start]);
                GetLongest(start,end-1,Nums[end]);
            }
        }

        Dict[num]= count;
        
        EvenCount = countSave.evenCount;
        OddCount = countSave.oddCount;
        
    }
}
